[00:10:19:04 --> 00:10:20:06] um latido.

#KAIROS E:0.0763 Pk:0.4394 Ph:0.0Hz V:0.0Hz R:3.91wps S:740ms

#KAIROS E:0.0956 Pk:0.5697 Ph:0.0Hz V:0.0Hz R:4.2wps S:639ms

#KAIROS E:0.0662 Pk:0.4211 Ph:0.0Hz V:0.0Hz R:2.75wps S:0ms
